## Phase 2: The option A covers (2026-11-07 to 2026-11-16; Daniel's approval after 2026-11-16)

Spec: "Phase 2: The option A covers" (Amendment 1, D-A1 to D-A11, FR-6 to FR-11, AC-6 to AC-12). Due: the final covers with a MANIFEST in `~/Desktop/print-ready/storytime/` by 2026-11-14, and in the 11-16 check folder for Daniel. The approved covers then go into the release proof of 2026-11-20, before the print order of 2026-11-25.

### Rules for every Phase 2 task

- Tests first. A Red task writes failing tests and commits them. Its Green task makes them pass and changes no test file. A review role reports and does not fix code.
- A task becomes `[x]` only with `commit <sha>` on the task line itself and its evidence class: owner-approved, person-checked, automated, or peer-report. A self-report closes no task.
- Mark a task `[~]` before you change a file outside `measure/`.
- A task that waits for Daniel stays `[ ]`. Use the word "deferred" only in the trailer `— deferred:<owner>`. In other text, write "later" or "waits for".
- Heavy jobs (vitest, tsc, Chrome, Ghostscript, PDF/X builds, contact sheets) run with ONE command: `HEAVY_MIN_AVAIL_MB=1500 HEAVY_PREFLIGHT_WAIT_S=5400 bash ~/Desktop/advantage-pr/scripts/jobs/heavy-run.sh -c '<command>'`. Never nest a heavy-run. Never use a plain `flock` on the lock inside a heavy job.
- Every dashboard vitest command has `--maxWorkers=1` (PM memory order, 2026-11-07).
- No pnpm in any form. No `npm install`. No `npm ci`. Run `git checkout -- pnpm-lock.yaml` after a gate if the lock file changed.
- Stage files path by path with `git commit -- <paths>`. Never stage `video/storytime-song/scripts/render.mjs`, `dashboard/raw.json`, or `measure/runs/**` of other tracks. No push, no amend, no reset, no revert, no stash.
- Guard G-1 (spec D-A3, review finding F1): no Phase 2 commit changes a shared file: the cover code of `dashboard/lib/storytime/print/`, the two old cover scripts, `content/storytime/covers/books.json`, every file under `dashboard/lib/covers/` (with `back-text.ts`), every file under `dashboard/scripts/print/` (with `make-pdfx.ts`), the other imports that D-A3 names, any other file that the new code imports, a week file, or anything under `video/`. Each changed path is in the allowed list of D-A3. Proof: `git show --name-only --format= <sha>` for each recorded SHA.
- Test names (spec D-A12, review finding F4): the Phase 2 test files are `dashboard/__tests__/quest-cover-template.test.ts` and `dashboard/__tests__/quest-cover-build.test.ts`. They are outside the shared globs `__tests__/storytime-*.test.ts` and `__tests__/covers-*.test.ts` of the print, loops, week-package, and unit 2 tracks. A red file of this track therefore fails no Green gate of another track. Rule R1 of `measure/tracks/storytime_teaching_loops_20261103/test-strategy.md` section 2.2 does not bind this track, and Red 1 can start at once. Section 2.3 of that file names this structural option. Guard G-1 keeps every shared file unchanged, so the other tracks lose no cover of a shared module.
- The reverse direction stays: the `PROJECT_TESTS` of this track holds the shared globs, so a red file of another track's open window fails it. A `PROJECT_TESTS` run of this track then passes when the failed tests are exactly the tests of that open Red file. The log names the failed tests and the open window (track, task, file). Each phase closeout runs `PROJECT_TESTS` with no open window of any track. Open windows (measured 2026-11-07 after commit f2c2a9c): the print track Red "test reliability" (`[~]`, its plan line 1287) and the week-package Phase 10 Red 2 (`storytime-package-family.test.ts`, commit 1fb10d7d; Green 2 `[~]`). The week-package Phase 10 Red 1 window closed with its Green 1 (commit ad767a78).
- No shared glob and no Red glob of another Storytime track (`storytime-print-*`, `storytime-loops-*`, `storytime-package-*`, `storytime-unit2-*`) matches `quest-cover-*`. The vitest default include finds `__tests__/quest-cover-*.test.ts` (`dashboard/vitest.config.ts` sets no `include`). Fast check before Red 1: `ls dashboard/__tests__/quest-cover-*.test.ts` finds no file (measured 2026-11-07).
- Date fallback (spec D-A13, review finding F4): if Green 2 (Task 2.7) has not passed by 2026-11-12 18:00 ICT, the final covers come from the option A mockup pipeline of `options/`. The steps: `build.py` with the fixed art of Task 2.8, Chrome through heavy-run, then `scripts/print/make-pdfx.ts`. The fallback writes the file names and the folders of spec D-A9. It keeps every print and check step of Tasks 2.9 to 2.12, and it changes no file under `dashboard/`. Tasks 2.4 to 2.7 then follow after 2026-11-14, and a later build replaces no approved file.
  - Checkpoint (`test-strategy.md` section 4.13): 2026-11-10 18:00 ICT. If Task 2.5 (Green 1) is not `[x]` then, the orchestrator starts the fallback driver in parallel with Tasks 2.5 to 2.7. Reason (measured): `options/build.py` builds only the 15 mockups. It has no teacher back, no loop over the 14 A3 covers and the 14 leaf covers, and no `--k-only` step.
  - The fallback driver and its checker `options/check_final.py` are new files under `options/`. The checker holds the checks of section 4.13 and catches its 5 mutants (log: `measure/runs/storytime_cover_options_20261106/mutants-fallback.txt`).
  - Trigger: at or after 2026-11-12 18:00 ICT, `grep -cE '^- \[x\] Task 2\.7 .*commit [0-9a-f]{7,40}' measure/tracks/storytime_cover_options_20261106/plan.md` prints `0`, or `GREEN_TEST_COMMAND` at HEAD exits non-zero.
  - Record: the orchestrator then writes the labeled line `Fallback D-A13 triggered: <date time ICT>; reason: <one line>` under this rule and keeps Task 2.7 open. AC-6 stays open until Tasks 2.4 to 2.7 pass.
- Pin note (`test-strategy.md` section 4.3): the orchestrator accepted the pins P1 to P12 with their defaults on 2026-11-07. This rule is the pin record. A Red or Green role changes a pin only with a new orchestrator line here. The orchestrator defaults:
  - `--version <n>` is the set number. The folder is `covers-quest-v<n>`. Set 1 gives student v4, teacher v2, and readers v1 (P5, spec D-A9).
  - The leaf print file adds `--k-only` to `--keep-fonts` (P7, spec FR-11.1).
  - The 11-16 copy mirrors the whole `covers-quest-v1` folder (section 4.12, spec D-A9).
  - The fallback checkpoint is 2026-11-10 18:00 ICT (section 4.13, the date fallback rule above).
  - Red 1 record (mid-red role, 2026-11-07): P1 to P12 read and accepted before the `[~]` mark of Task 2.4. No pin changed. The Red 1 file uses the export names of P1, the render rules of P2 and P3, the ppi rule of P10, the band mapping of P11, and the test timeouts of P12.
- Status line (`test-strategy.md` section 5 item 13 and section 4.14 step 9): the note of this track in `measure/tracks.md` and in `metadata.json` reads "Phase 2 in progress; waits for Daniel's check of 2026-11-16" until Task 2.14. No text calls these covers "approved" before Task 2.14.
- The repository is public. Thai goes to Daniel only. No Thai goes to another model. No new Thai.
- Large binary files stay out of git: the art lives in `content/storytime/l2/print/pictures/` (`.gitignore` line 37), the builds in `~/Desktop/print-ready/storytime/covers-quest-v1/`.
- This repo has no `measure/generate.sh` or `measure/doctor.sh`. The doctor step of the phase is the `PROJECT_CHECKS` gate.
- Standing cover rules (PM standing order 2026-11-11, word for word): "PM → Workbooks: STANDING ORDER (PM, 2026-11-11, from the freshness pass; it applies to every cover, poster, and print-chrome task from now). The reader covers took 3 Red + Green + rebuild rounds today (r2, r2b, r2c) because the PM look and the Red pins checked the rules one element at a time. From now, the Red of any cover or chrome change pins Daniel's 5 cover rules on EVERY cover kind (book front/back, A3, leaf, teacher) and EVERY chrome element (logo tile, plaque, pill, bar, footer): 1. the product logo is present (by file hash); 2. the chrome colors come from the logo token table (Storytime: #fcd34d / #92400e; K-only pages: K tints); 3. the visible letters (cap height, not the line box) fill about 80 % (75 % or more) of their band; 4. each text block is centered within 1 mm, front and back; 5. 0 band words, 0 band icons, 0 XP or game bars. The sheet you send carries one full-size page per cover kind. Put this in your plan's standing rules. No reply needed beyond your receipt." The Red of any cover or chrome change pins the 5 rules on every cover kind and every chrome element in the shipped style (test file dashboard/__tests__/quest-cover-standing-rules.test.ts), and every sheet to the PM carries one full-size page per cover kind.

### Gate commands (orchestrator environment)

```bash
RED_TEST_COMMAND='cd dashboard && CI=true npx vitest run __tests__/quest-cover-*.test.ts --maxWorkers=1'    # must fail after each Red, before its Green, and only in the new Red file
GREEN_TEST_COMMAND='cd dashboard && CI=true npx vitest run __tests__/quest-cover-*.test.ts --maxWorkers=1'  # must pass after Green
PROJECT_TESTS='cd dashboard && CI=true npx vitest run __tests__/storytime-*.test.ts __tests__/covers-*.test.ts __tests__/print-pdfx.test.ts __tests__/quest-cover-*.test.ts --maxWorkers=1'   # the shared list plus the files of this track (spec D-A12)
PROJECT_LINT='cd dashboard && npx eslint lib/storytime scripts/storytime __tests__/quest-cover-*.test.ts'
PROJECT_CHECKS='cd dashboard && npx tsx scripts/storytime/check-weeks.ts --level 2 && npx tsc --noEmit'  # the form of the print track since its review round 8 (F52): check-weeks exits 1 on any FAIL row, so a FAIL or a crash fails the gate; the whole command runs inside one heavy-run wrapper; no nested heavy-run
UX_REQUIRED=never   # print files: the visual checks are the look task and Daniel's check
PRINT_GATE='cd dashboard && npx tsx scripts/print/make-pdfx.ts <print file> --check'   # exit 0 for each of the 4 print files
PRINT_TEXT_CHECK='f=<print file>; for p in $(seq 1 <pages>); do printf "page %s: " "$p"; pdftotext -f "$p" -l "$p" "$f" - | tr -s "[:space:]" " " | grep -o "Storytime Advantage" | wc -l; done; pdffonts "$f"'   # next to PRINT_GATE, for each of the 4 print files (test-strategy.md section 4.10, checks 3 and 4); <pages> is 2, 2, 14, 14
```

The whole command of each gate runs inside one heavy-run wrapper. `PROJECT_TESTS` is the list of the print track plus `__tests__/quest-cover-*.test.ts`, so a change in a shared module shows up there. This track changes no week file, so `check-weeks.ts` must give the same rows before and after each Green. If `PROJECT_TESTS` fails only in a test of another track that this track did not touch, the orchestrator records the failed test names and asks that track's session. This track does not edit that test. The rule for an open Red window of another track is in the rules above.

`PRINT_TEXT_CHECK` runs next to `PRINT_GATE`, because `checkPdfx` excuses a page that already is one page-size image (`test-strategy.md` F12). Every cover page has a full-page art image, so a rasterized page passes `PRINT_GATE`. The check passes when each `page <n>:` line prints at least `1`. Also, `pdffonts` must list at least 1 font, each with `emb` `yes`, and no font of the type `Type 3`.

The check joins the white space before the match. Measured on 2026-11-07: `pdftotext` puts "Storytime" and "Advantage" of the front banner on two lines. On the option A student front and teacher front mockups, the match gives 0 without the join and 1 with the join. The join form is not measured on a PDF/X file yet; Task 2.9 is its first run. The Thai byte check reads the Chrome PDFs (`student/cover.pdf`, `teacher/teacher-cover.pdf`) or the HTML, never a PDF/X file. Reason: `pdftotext` of the PDF/X file drops the Thai tone marks (`test-strategy.md` F13).

Baseline (`test-strategy.md` section 4.19; no new run before Red 1): `measure/runs/storytime_week_package_20261022/p10-green1-project.txt` at ad767a78 has 60 files and 1269 tests, exit 0, in 1145 s with `--maxWorkers=1`. The Green 1 gate expects these counts, and the Green 1 log records the equation and the real numbers:

- files = 60 + the test files that other tracks added after ad767a78 (`git diff --name-status ad767a78..HEAD -- dashboard/__tests__`) + the untracked test files of open windows + 1 (`quest-cover-template.test.ts`);
- tests = 1269 + the tests of those files + the test count of Red 1.

A difference without a cause stops the gate. A new baseline run is necessary only when a commit of another track changes `dashboard/lib` or `dashboard/scripts` before Green 1. The command is `git log --oneline ad767a78..HEAD -- dashboard/lib dashboard/scripts` (0 commits on 2026-11-07 after commit f2c2a9c). On that date, the diff of `dashboard/__tests__` adds 1 file: `storytime-package-family.test.ts` (commit 1fb10d7d).
